+def _attach_direct_factor_model_features(frame: pd.DataFrame, direct_result: Any) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, list[str]]:
+    selected_candidate = str(direct_result.summary.get("selected_candidate", {}).get("candidate", ""))
+    positions = direct_result.positions.copy()
+    if not selected_candidate or positions.empty:
+        out = frame.copy()
+        out[DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL] = 0.0
+        out[DIRECT_FACTOR_RAW_TARGET_COL] = 0.0
+        return out, [DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL, DIRECT_FACTOR_RAW_TARGET_COL]
+
+    selected = positions.loc[positions["candidate"].astype(str).eq(selected_candidate)].copy()
+    selected["datetime"] = pd.to_datetime(selected["datetime"]).dt.normalize()
+    selected = selected[["datetime", "target_position", "raw_target"]].rename(
+        columns={
+            "target_position": DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL,
+            "raw_target": DIRECT_FACTOR_RAW_TARGET_COL,
+        }
+    )
+    out = frame.copy()
+    out["datetime"] = pd.to_datetime(out["datetime"]).dt.normalize()
+    out = out.merge(selected, on="datetime", how="left")
+    out[DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL] = (
+        pd.to_numeric(out[DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL], errors="coerce")
+        .replace([float("inf"), float("-inf")], pd.NA)
+        .fillna(0.0)
+        .clip(lower=0.0, upper=1.0)
+    )
+    out[DIRECT_FACTOR_RAW_TARGET_COL] = (
+        pd.to_numeric(out[DIRECT_FACTOR_RAW_TARGET_COL], errors="coerce")
+        .replace([float("inf"), float("-inf")], pd.NA)
+        .fillna(0.0)
+    )
+    selected_base = out[DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL]
+    selected_raw = out[DIRECT_FACTOR_RAW_TARGET_COL]
+    if selected_base.abs().mean() <= 1e-9 and selected_raw.abs().mean() > 1e-9:
+        out[DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL] = _direct_factor_base_position_from_raw(selected_raw)
+    return out, [DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL, DIRECT_FACTOR_RAW_TARGET_COL]
+
+
+def _direct_factor_base_position_from_raw(raw_target: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
+    raw = pd.to_numeric(raw_target, errors="coerce").replace([float("inf"), float("-inf")], pd.NA).fillna(0.0)
+    max_abs = float(raw.abs().max())
+    if max_abs <= 1e-12:
+        return pd.Series(0.0, index=raw.index, name=DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL)
+    if float(raw.max()) <= 1e-12 and float(raw.min()) < -1e-12:
+        normalized = (-raw / max_abs).clip(lower=0.0, upper=1.0)
+    else:
+        normalized = (raw / max_abs).clip(lower=0.0, upper=1.0)
+    return normalized.rename(DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL)
+
+
 def _load_benchmarks(root: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
+    open2open_path = (
+        root / "research" / "agentic_alpha_high_chop_open2open_lgb_xgb_v1" / "backtest_benchmark_comparison.csv"
+    )
+    if open2open_path.exists():
+        frame = pd.read_csv(open2open_path)
+        aliases = {
+            "legacy_soxlbacktest_reference": "soxl_backtest",
+            "base_constrained_ref": "base_constrained_ref",
+            "soxl_buyhold": "soxl_buyhold",
+        }
+        rows = []
+        for row in frame.to_dict(orient="records"):
+            strategy = str(row["strategy"])
+            name = aliases.get(strategy)
+            if name is None:
+                continue
+            rows.append(
+                {
+                    "name": name,
+                    "return_pct": _float_or_zero(row.get("return_pct")),
+                    "max_drawdown_pct": _float_or_zero(row.get("max_drawdown_pct")),
+                    "trade_count": _int_or_zero(row.get("trade_count")),
+                    "source": f"open2open:{strategy}",
+                }
+            )
+            if name == "base_constrained_ref":
+                rows.append(
+                    {
+                        "name": "constrained_weight_reference",
+                        "return_pct": _float_or_zero(row.get("return_pct")),
+                        "max_drawdown_pct": _float_or_zero(row.get("max_drawdown_pct")),
+                        "trade_count": _int_or_zero(row.get("trade_count")),
+                        "source": "open2open:base_constrained_ref_alias",
+                    }
+                )
+        if rows:
+            return rows

+def test_cognitive_alpha_goal_codex_exec_attaches_direct_factor_base_to_model_frame():
+    from src.workflow.cognitive_alpha_goal_codex_exec import (
+        DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL,
+        DIRECT_FACTOR_RAW_TARGET_COL,
+        _attach_direct_factor_model_features,
+    )
+
+    frame = pd.DataFrame(
+        {
+            "datetime": pd.to_datetime(["2026-01-02", "2026-01-05", "2026-01-06"]),
+            "forward_return": [0.02, -0.01, 0.03],
+        }
+    )
+    direct_result = SimpleNamespace(
+        summary={"selected_candidate": {"candidate": "direct_winner"}},
+        positions=pd.DataFrame(
+            {
+                "datetime": pd.to_datetime(
+                    [
+                        "2026-01-02",
+                        "2026-01-05",
+                        "2026-01-06",
+                        "2026-01-02",
+                        "2026-01-05",
+                        "2026-01-06",
+                    ]
+                ),
+                "candidate": ["direct_winner", "direct_winner", "direct_winner", "other", "other", "other"],
+                "raw_target": [0.1, 0.8, 0.3, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0],
+                "target_position": [0.0, 0.7, 0.7, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0],
+            }
+        ),
+    )
+
+    out, direct_feature_cols = _attach_direct_factor_model_features(frame, direct_result)
+
+    assert direct_feature_cols == [DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL, DIRECT_FACTOR_RAW_TARGET_COL]
+    assert out[DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL].tolist() == [0.0, 0.7, 0.7]
+    assert out[DIRECT_FACTOR_RAW_TARGET_COL].tolist() == [0.1, 0.8, 0.3]
+

+def test_cognitive_alpha_goal_codex_exec_uses_open_to_open_training_and_lgb_xgb():
+    from src.workflow.cognitive_alpha_goal_codex_exec import (
+        LABEL_OPEN_TO_OPEN_5D_COL,
+        OPEN_TO_OPEN_RETURN_COL,
+        CognitiveAlphaGoalCodexExecCardSpec,
+        _add_return_columns,
+        _position_spec,
+    )
+
+    frame = pd.DataFrame(
+        {
+            "datetime": pd.date_range("2026-01-02", periods=8, freq="B"),
+            "open": [10.0, 11.0, 12.1, 10.89, 11.4345, 12.006225, 11.40591375, 12.546505125],
+            "close": [10.5, 11.2, 12.0, 11.0, 11.6, 12.2, 11.4, 12.4],
+        }
+    )
+
+    _add_return_columns(frame)
+
+    assert frame.loc[0, OPEN_TO_OPEN_RETURN_COL] == pytest.approx(12.1 / 11.0 - 1.0)
+    assert frame.loc[0, LABEL_OPEN_TO_OPEN_5D_COL] == pytest.approx(11.40591375 / 11.0 - 1.0)
+    assert frame.loc[frame.index.max(), OPEN_TO_OPEN_RETURN_COL] == 0.0
+    assert pd.isna(frame.loc[frame.index.max(), LABEL_OPEN_TO_OPEN_5D_COL])
+
+    spec = CognitiveAlphaGoalCodexExecCardSpec(root=ROOT)
+    position_spec = _position_spec(["feature_a"], spec=spec, test=("2026-01-02", "2026-07-02"))
+
+    model_families = {item["model"] for item in position_spec.model_specs}
+    assert {"lgbm_classifier", "lgbm_regressor", "xgb_classifier", "xgb_regressor"}.issubset(model_families)
+    assert position_spec.return_col == OPEN_TO_OPEN_RETURN_COL
+    assert position_spec.label_col == LABEL_OPEN_TO_OPEN_5D_COL
+    assert position_spec.constrained_reference_name == "base_constrained_ref"
+

+def test_cognitive_alpha_goal_codex_exec_normalizes_clipped_negative_direct_factor_raw_target():
+    from src.workflow.cognitive_alpha_goal_codex_exec import (
+        DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL,
+        DIRECT_FACTOR_RAW_TARGET_COL,
+        _attach_direct_factor_model_features,
+    )
+
+    frame = pd.DataFrame(
+        {
+            "datetime": pd.to_datetime(["2026-01-02", "2026-01-05", "2026-01-06"]),
+            "open_to_open_return": [0.02, -0.01, 0.0],
+        }
+    )
+    direct_result = SimpleNamespace(
+        summary={"selected_candidate": {"candidate": "negative_direct_winner"}},
+        positions=pd.DataFrame(
+            {
+                "datetime": pd.to_datetime(["2026-01-02", "2026-01-05", "2026-01-06"]),
+                "candidate": ["negative_direct_winner"] * 3,
+                "raw_target": [-1.0, -0.5, 0.0],
+                "target_position": [0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
+            }
+        ),
+    )
+
+    out, _ = _attach_direct_factor_model_features(frame, direct_result)
+
+    assert out[DIRECT_FACTOR_RAW_TARGET_COL].tolist() == [-1.0, -0.5, 0.0]
+    assert out[DIRECT_FACTOR_BASE_POSITION_COL].tolist() == pytest.approx([1.0, 0.5, 0.0])
